# Image credits

1 historical image, checked on 2026-10-31, plus the files made by this project.

Files are at most 1600 px wide. "Resized" is the change made to the file.

| File | Title, date | Source | Licence | Attribution to show | Used in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `site/assets/img/fanghu-shengjing-1744.jpg`, `-800.jpg` | 方壶胜境 Fanghu Shengjing, "Beautiful Scene of the Square Pot", leaf of the Forty Scenes album (圆明园四十景图), commissioned 1744. Painting on silk. Resized from 1680 × 1688 px. | [Wikimedia Commons, File:Beautiful Scene of the Square Pot.jpg](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Beautiful_Scene_of_the_Square_Pot.jpg). The file page gives MIT OpenCourseWare / Visualizing Cultures as the upload source. Album held by the Bibliothèque nationale de France. | Public domain: file page tagged `{{PD-Art|PD-old-100-1923}}` | 沈源 Shen Yuan and 唐岱 Tangdai, painters; 汪由敦 Wang Youdun, calligrapher | Homepage hero (EN, ZH), credits |

## Notes

1. **Licence tags differ across the album on Commons.** This leaf is tagged PD-Art. Other leaves checked on the same day (for example `Hall of Rectitude and Honor.jpg` and `Calm Lake Under the Autumn Moon.jpg`) carry a CC BY-SA 3.0 tag from the uploader. Check each leaf's own page before using it.
2. **BnF.** The scan's path to Commons runs through MIT, not Gallica, and its origin before that is not stated. The BnF / Gallica credit line is not used for this file. If a Gallica copy replaces it, the credit "Source gallica.bnf.fr / BnF" applies.

## Files made by this project

- `site/assets/mark.svg`, `site/assets/favicon.svg`, `site/favicon.ico`, `site/apple-touch-icon.png`: a roof over a hall and a water line.

## Fonts and the family mark

- `site/assets/yaga-mark-dark.webp`: the yaga.sh family mark, copied from the Küstrin site.
- Geist, Geist Mono, Newsreader (copied from the Küstrin site) and subsets of Noto Sans SC and Noto Serif SC: SIL Open Font License.
